# Predictions ledger (protocol v2 §4)

One row per prediction made before a timed run. Rules: `research/architecture/measurement-protocol-v2.md` §4
(prediction card: build + base + hashes, driver, metric, baseline, closure check, term breakdown, predicted delta with
interval, falsification reading, decision). A model with **two misses** (outside its interval) is reviewed by its
author's team before it drives another build. Kept by mgmt-ops; owners send their prediction card before the run and
the measured result after it.

Columns: **id** · author · date (UTC) · build vs base · driver (S scroll / W window / M morph driver / G gesture /
F finger, §5.4) · metric · predicted [interval] · measured median [range or IQR] · in interval? · error ratio
(measured / predicted delta) · lesson.

Rows before 2026-10-01 ~10:45Z were made before protocol v2: their predictions were stated in messages or notes, not
on a prediction card, and the runs were n=3 (not 4v4). They are recorded so the two-miss rule counts them.

Readout per rotator tick (L11571 TimerFire): ≥10 ms whole-view Layout in the next frame, excluding ~0.1 ms carousel-rooted post-paint Layouts. Reading: C view + V1 gone (≥75 % of ticks no ≥10 ms view Layout, or carousel-rooted) -> W2 (img swap is the escape; direction fixed slots + src/transform rotation); C view + V1 view-rooted -> W3 or another tick dirtier, read rotoff; V2 #people-rooted at ≤ half C -> boundary holds; V2 view-rooted -> escape outside #people or no boundary on this WebKit; C not view-rooted -> stop. Prediction: V1 removes the ≥10 ms tick Layouts (W2); V2 #people-rooted ≤ half; headline ≥101 ms y≈1960 rebuild frame NOT tested here. Relabel: the 398x52 root (countdown 1 s tick) = fixed top nav `.start-selling-top-nav` (16,16)+scrollY, not a countdown strip. SECOND READ (mgmt-ops-5 16:3xZ): PENDING 3 items: (1) confound, does noswap keep the exit width steps (W3)? if not, "V1 gone" = W2 or W3, not separated; (2) grey zone: still view-rooted = ≥50 % of ticks, 50-75 % = MIXED (no direction, read rotoff), plus min ticks per run; (3) "idle dt>34 drops by the rotator share" needs a number + scoring runs or is info-only. Slot: right after P-032. 16:4xZ ANSWERS (fix-scroll-engine README 894dfbf): (1) none of the P14 builds has exit width steps (aw1-10e, re-keep, re-contain-js, st4-noswap all carry the layout-animations rewrite: avatars-exit opacity/translate/clip-path/transform, avatars-shift translate, avatars-pop; 0 width/flex-basis/margin keyframe hits; only the original baseline has width steps), so "W3 = exit width steps" was a mislabel; noswap also drops those animations (they match img.is-exit/.is-enter inside `ge`, never mounted). Reworded reading: V1 gone -> escape is in the swap path (`ge` mount/unmount: renderer insert/remove OR the animations' layer changes, NOT separated), direction = a rotator with no keyed remount (a split arm, `ge` mounted with animation:none, is a later block only if it matters); V1 view -> something noswap keeps (S/x+1 state writes, in-place src update, or a pending full layout), read rotoff. c[4]-c[7] backfilled from the signed card on the lead's order) | Registered text of the signed card (fixes/end-bg/README.md section 8, rev 1 at 46afb577; tables flattened to text; lead-ordered backfill): - Question. On the end screen of C, the nobg arm cut phone slow frames 16 -> 3 (P-137, CAUSE). nobg removes two things: the stage pictures, grain and blend layers (pictures-off, pic), and the gradients of three sweeping row titles, doc, earn and secure (titles-off, tit). Which of the two carries the paint cost of a toolbar step there? - Why it matters. PR-025's option C (baked backgrounds) removes only the pictures. If the titles carry a real share, they get their own topic (end-titles, a proposal or a pixel-identical compositor version) and the user is told C alone is not expected to remove the lag. Chrome cannot answer it (section 7, F-289). - What is known (our data only): - P-137 (phone G, xcode-serious): nobg CAUSE at >34 (C median 16, V 3); MIXED-class slow frames vanish. - Chrome census (F-289): paint counts per window equal in C, pic, tit and nobg; no durations. - WebKit (section 5, helper read of WebKit main a1c4dcf7, partly CONFIRMED): the paint walk runs on the WebContent main thread in the rendering update; on iOS DOM rendering goes to the GPU process by default, so the main thread records draw commands and the GPU process rasterizes. Risk named before data: WebKit Timeline's Paint records on the WebContent side may show the recording only, not the raster of a picture. Then M1 cannot see nobg's effect, the control fails, and the card ends CONTROL-FAILED (the outcome that sends the question to the phone ticket). - Base. main as of d9dabfc7 (this branch rebased on it). C is served from the fixes/end-bg overlay: v9-final + morph-scroll rev 7d, JS 1b2eecc66011, CSS e1359ab9994a (the bytes P-137 measured as C). The driver checks the served hashes and x-mirror-source: overlay through the http twin before every hold (exit 26). - Arms (eb-inject.mjs, appended as one <style id="eb-arm"> after every page load, before the Timeline starts; paint-only properties: background-image, mix-blend-mode, visibility; the self-test checks it): arm - CSS - asks; C - none - control; C2 - none (C under another label) - A/A pair: the instrument's noise; nob - nobg.css d566af712894 (P-137's V) - positive control, in every hold; pic - sim/pictures-off.css (nobg minus its six title lines) - pictures alone; tit - sim/titles-off.css (the six title lines: doc, earn, secure and their ::after) - the three titles alone; pic + tit = nob, selector for selector (self-test). The organized title's sweep and every title's mask and animation stay in all arms. All arms change the look: mechanism only, never shipped, no pixel check. - Applied probes (counts and booleans, read at the end of each run): the stage grain's background (.start-selling-platform-stage-grain) and the secure title's (.is-payment .start-selling-platform-row-title-secure, span or ::after) must be on/off as the arm says, and the style element present only in nob/pic/tit. A run that fails is INSUFFICIENT; a C that fails fails the replication gate. - Stimulus end-cycle-v1.json (compiler hash c9e62ddadacc): reload, 3 s idle, drags of 520 px down the page until the end (a runtime loop, cap 20), then 6 cycles at the end: a finger-down drag of 400 px (the page goes up, the toolbar expands) and a finger-up drag of 440 px (back to the end, the toolbar collapses). Drags end at rest. On this build docH is about 6740 and max scroll about 5964, so every cycle stays in the last-screen band. Planned toolbar steps in the band: 12 per run. - Order: three holds of 5 steps, each with its own C pair and its own nob; pic, tit and C2 twice each, in two holds, in rotated positions: hold - steps; E1 - C:H1 nob:H1 pic:H1 tit:H1 C:H2; E2 - C:H3 tit:H2 nob:H2 C2:H1 C:H4; E3 - C:H5 C2:H2 pic:H2 nob:H3 C:H6; The driver accepts only these holds with exactly these steps (exit 2 otherwise). - Pins (sha256/12): - stimulus sim/end-cycle-v1.json 258b9dadae1e (compiled program c9e62ddadacc); - inject sim/eb-inject.mjs 20b18b4de87a; rules and filter sim/p144-lib.mjs 5cc29ba45734; - driver sim/sim-p144.mjs a86d5b205362; dry run sim/dry-p144.mjs 3ed80224b792; check sim/check-p144.mjs 8e44588002ce; scorer sim/score-p144.mjs f1c45c4e292b; - self-test sim/p144-selftest.mjs c8402de03ae7: SELFTEST ok 87 checks (the stimulus compiles to one approach loop and 12 guarded cycle strokes; pic + tit = nob selector for selector, disjoint, paint-only properties; the applied rule per arm; both plans: C bracket, nob in every hold, pic/tit/C2 in two holds; the budget and host thresholds; the band filter and the per-frame metrics on a synthetic record; every call, gate, reading and the metric switch; the dry-run, n-check and first-run gates); - arm CSS: nobg.css d566af712894, sim/pictures-off.css 2f316f33d828, sim/titles-off.css 6bc2f3c17007; - served build: JS 1b2eecc66011, CSS e1359ab9994a. | Registered text of the signed card (section 8, rev 1 at 46afb577): - Instrument and filter (p144-lib.mjs, frozen): - Frames: the longest recorded segment; a frame is BAND-MOVING when innerHeight is strictly between the run's two toolbar states and scrollY >= docH - 2 innerHeight (P-137's last-screen band). n = such frames; a run counts at n >= 8. - A Timeline record belongs to a frame when it starts in (t - dt, t]. - M1 (primary): per frame, the summed duration of the Paint records (WebKit nests them in Composite); per run, the median over the band-moving frames. - M2 (pre-registered fallback): the same with the Composite records (the layer flush that contains the paint walk). M2 replaces M1 for the whole card only when M1's control (or replication) fails in every hold and M2 replicates with its control passing in every hold. Otherwise M2 is reported, never read. - Descriptive only: Paint records per frame, painted area (clip quads). - Ratio = arm metric / mean metric of the nearest C before and after it in its hold. LOCATES <= 0.30, PARTIAL <= 0.70, NONE otherwise (P-133's bands). | Registered text of the signed card (section 8, rev 1 at 46afb577): - Dry run (count-only, after the signature, before any Timeline data). dry-p144.mjs, one shared Simulator slot, no Timeline, no timing number: one C run and one nob run with the logger. A run passes when: no abort or error; the approach loop ended at the end of the page; 12 cycle strokes; the logger saw >= 8 toolbar steps in the band; the arm's probes are right. It times each step (open + gesture). Rule, from the mean step s (estimate = s + 10): - estimate x 5 + 30 <= 500 s: plan E above; - else estimate x 4 + 30 <= 500 s: plan F, six holds of 4 steps (C nob X C / C X nob C, X = pic, tit, C2 twice each, nob in every hold; p144-lib.mjs PLAN4); - else: the design fails its budget; no data; back to the ops read and the lead. A failing run: DRY FAIL, no Timeline data, the script is revised and the card goes back to the read. - Holds. Normal exclusive holds, --max-seconds 600 --wait 14400, one FIFO entry at a time, in order, in the background; SB_BUDGET_S=500; DEFERRED while a phone block is marked. Hold E1 (the others the same with their steps): node tools/lock.mjs run chrome-measure --max-seconds 600 --wait 14400 --owner fix-end-bg -- sh -c \ 'node tools/fix.mjs serve end-bg > out/end-bg/serve-p144.log 2>&1 & S=$!; \ node tools/wait.mjs serve out/serve-end-bg.json --pid $S --owner fix-end-bg --timeout 60 && \ SB_BUDGET_S=500 node tools/sim.mjs session --owner fix-end-bg -- \ node fixes/end-bg/sim/sim-p144.mjs E1 C:H1 nob:H1 pic:H1 tit:H1 C:H2; E=$?; kill $S; exit $E' The driver: served bytes (26); per step the host rule, sim.mjs open, sim.mjs gesture end-cycle-v1.json --timeline --inject eb-inject.mjs; the first-run gate after the first step (C applied, docH0 > 0, n >= 8; else 27); the budget stop (dropped steps logged, not scored). - Host-load rule (P-141 rev 1.1's, unchanged): load1 > 200 at a step start exits 28 and the hold is VOID-HOST. Its folder moves to <hold>-void1 (kept, reported, never scored) and the hold re-runs once from an empty folder; a second VOID-HOST of one hold stops the card. 200 is the lead's P-141 value, set from P-133's step-start loads on this host, not from any P-144 number (there is none). load1 at every step's start and end is logged and reported. - Per hold, right after it (before anything is scored): check-p144.mjs out/end-bg/simp144 <hold> --json fixes/end-bg/sim/p144-check-<hold>.json. HOLD-FAIL (exit 3) when a run that ran has no Timeline file, an abort, an error, an approach loop not ended at the end, cycle strokes != 12, or n < 8: the hold is not scored, no rescue, and the remaining holds stop (to the lead). Reported, never failing: n below 12, band toolbar steps, docH, css sha. - Stop rules (P-141 N3's runner): exit 0 -> the check, then the next hold; exit 28 -> the void folder, one re-run; any other non-zero (26, 27, 2, 124, a lock or wait timeout) or HOLD-FAIL -> stop the remaining holds, to the lead. A later hold runs whatever the earlier holds' values are; nothing is scored before the last hold. - Scoring (after the last hold): score-p144.mjs out/end-bg/simp144 E1 E2 E3 --json fixes/end-bg/sim/p144-score.json. - Rules (frozen before data, p144-lib.mjs scoreHold / armCall / cardReading): 1. NOT REPRODUCED when any hold's C runs fail the replication gate: every C applied, counted, metric > 0 and Paint in at least half its band-moving frames. No arm is judged. 2. CONTROL-FAILED per hold: nob not LOCATES or PARTIAL; that hold's other arms are not read (reported). In every hold: the card status is CONTROL-FAILED, which is the "Simulator can't split them" outcome. 3. VOID per hold: abs(last C - first C) / first C > 0.30; its arms are not read. 4. A/A: any C2 run PARTIAL or LOCATES in a read hold: AA-FAILED; no pic/tit reading (reported only). Each C2 ratio's distance from 1 is reported as the noise beside the calls. 5. GEOMETRY: an arm whose final docH differs from a neighbouring C's by > 2 px is not judged. 6. Calls per arm over its runs in read holds: LOCATES when both LOCATE or the mean ratio <= 0.30; PARTIAL when the mean <= 0.70; NONE otherwise; one valid run: its call, marked "one run". 7. Reading (rules 1-4 passed): pic LOCATES/PARTIAL and tit NONE: PICTURES (no end-titles topic; PR-025's option C is the lever on this instrument). tit LOCATES/PARTIAL and pic NONE: TITLES (open end-titles; tell the lead and the user that C alone is not expected to remove the lag). Both: BOTH (open end-titles too; both shares reported). Neither while nob passes: JOINT (only removing both helps, or the split is not additive; to the lead, no topic). 8. A Simulator ratio is never a phone result and never a gain. Descriptive: (1 - pic) + (1 - tit) vs (1 - nob). Scoring precedence (P-141 N5 style): rule 1 fails: P3 a miss, P4-P8 insufficient. CONTROL-FAILED card: P4 a miss, P5 a miss, P6-P8 insufficient. AA-FAILED: P9 a miss, P6-P8 insufficient. PARTIAL counts as a hit for P6 and P7. | Registered text of the signed card (section 8, rev 1 at 46afb577; table flattened; p = probability): - Predictions (registered before data; informed by P-137's result and the Chrome census, the only data seen on these arms): # - prediction - p; 1 - the dry run passes and picks plan E - 65 %; 2 - all holds pass the n check - 80 %; 3 - C replicates on M1 in every hold - 70 %; 4 - nob LOCATES or PARTIAL on M1 in every hold (no metric switch) - 45 %; 5 - the card ends READ (on M1 or M2) - 50 %; 6 - given READ: pic LOCATES or PARTIAL - 75 %; 7 - given READ: tit LOCATES or PARTIAL - 30 %; 8 - given READ: the reading is PICTURES - 55 %; 9 - both C2 runs NONE - 85 %; 10 - no arm GEOMETRY - 90 %; 11 - every run passes the applied probes - 85 %; 12 - no VOID-HOST hold - 75 %; | Simulator Timeline, plan E, holds E1-E3 16:11-16:52Z, every exit 0, HOLD-OK x3, no VOID-HOST (step-start load1 7.7-106.4), no dropped step, no void folder; n 100-137 per run; every run applied (probes right, arm CSS sha per arm); docH 6814 to 6850 in all, no GEOMETRY. M1 Paint ms per band frame C 0.528-0.638; ratios nob 0.882 / 0.874 / 0.860 NONE, pic 0.696 PARTIAL (E1) / 0.920 NONE (E3), tit 0.895 / 0.902 NONE, C2 1.012 / 0.957 NONE (all unread: holds CONTROL-FAILED). C drift M1 0.172 / 0.031 / 0.023 (VOID line 0.30), replication gate passed in every hold. M2 Composite (reported, not read): nob 0.685 PARTIAL / 0.785 / 0.768 NONE, so the control passes in E1 only and the switch (M1 fails in every hold AND M2 control passes in every hold) does not apply. Status CONTROL-FAILED on M1, no reading. Descriptive, OBSERVED: Paint about 0.5 ms per frame vs Composite 15-24 ms, 12-13 Paint records and about 0.83-0.96 million px per frame in every arm | P1 Y, P2 Y, P3 Y, P4 N (informed, N4), P5 N (informed via the M1/M2 choice, N4), P6 -, P7 -, P8 -, P9 Y (N1), P10 Y, P11 Y, P12 Y | 7 hit, 2 miss (P4, P5), 3 insufficient (P6-P8) by N3 precedence (a CONTROL-FAILED card makes P4 and P5 misses and P6-P8 insufficient); P4 and P5 are two misses on one model (Simulator Paint records see nobg's effect). Verdict CONFIRMED: CONTROL-FAILED, the pre-named outcome; the Simulator cannot split pictures from titles on this instrument; the question goes to the phone Timeline ticket per the lead's ruling | WebKit Timeline Paint on the WebContent side is too small a share of the frame (about 0.5 of 15-24 ms) to carry a paint-cost attribution on the Simulator (OBSERVED, cause unknown; the pre-named raster-outside-Paint risk fits but is unconfirmed); the scratch read N4 disclosed (Paint 0.28 s vs 13 s Composite) already pointed there, so an informed pre-read of the metric share should set the control's metric, not a 45 % hope; a fallback that needs every hold can be one hold short, as here (M2 control in E1 only) |
